Entry91/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 147
English
Isnad

From Muhammad b. Maysara, from Muhammad al-Zuhri, from Sa'id b. al-Musayyab, from 'Umar b. al-Khattab, from 'Umar b. al-Khattab, who said:

Matn

“O Messenger of God, do you see this deed of ours: is it for something already finished with, or for something we begin anew?” He said: “Rather, for something already finished with.” 'Umar said: “Then what is the use of action?” The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Act, for each will attain only through action.” 'Umar said: “That is when we strive.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ مَيْسَرَةَ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدٍ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، عَنْ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ، قَالَ:

Matn

يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ، أَرَأَيْتَ عَمَلَنَا هَذَا لِمَا قَدْ فُرِغَ مِنْهُ أَوْ لِمَا يُأْتَنَفُ؟ قَالَ: «بَلْ لِمَا قَدْ فُرِغَ مِنْهُ» . قَالَ عُمَرُ: فَفِيمَا الْعَمَلُ إِذَنْ؟ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «اعْمَلُوا فَإِنَّ كَلًّا لَا يَنَالُ إِلَّا بِعَمَلٍ» . فَقَالَ عُمَرُ: ذَاكَ حِينَ نَجْتَهِدُ

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01'Umar b. al-KhattabEarliest transmitterعمر بن الخطاب
  2. 02Sa'id b. al-Musayyabسعيد بن المسيب
  3. 03Muhammad al-Zuhriمحمد الزهري
  4. 04Muhammad b. Maysaraمحمد بن ميسره
  5. 05Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

Entry92/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 147
English
Isnad

From Hisham, companion of al-Dastuwa'i, from Yahya b. Abi Kathir, from Muhammad b. 'Abd al-Rahman b. Thawban, from Abu Rifa'a, from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri, who said:

Matn

A man came to the Messenger of God ﷺ and said: “I have a slave girl from whom I practice 'azl; I want what men want, but I dislike that she conceive, and the Jews claim that 'azl is the lesser burying alive (al-maw'uda al-sughra).” He said: “The Jews lie. If God wanted to create it, you could not turn it away.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ هِشَامٍ، صَاحِبِ الدَّسْتُوَائِيِّ ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ أَبِي كَثِيرٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ ثَوْبَانَ، عَنْ أَبِي رِفَاعَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، قَالَ:

Matn

جَاءَ رَجُلٌ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ فَقَالَ: إِنَّ لِي وَلِيدَةً أَعْزِلُ عَنْهَا ، وَأَنَا أُرِيدُ مَا يُرِيدُ الرَّجُلُ ، وَأَنَا أَكْرَهُ أَنْ تَحْمِلَ ، وَتَزْعُمُ يَهُودُ أَنَّ الْعَزْلَ الْمَوْءُودَةُ الصُّغْرَى؟ فَقَالَ: «كَذِبَتْ يَهُودُ، لَوْ أَرَادَ اللَّهُ أَنْ يَخْلُقَهُ لَمْ تَسْتَطِعْ أَنْ تَصْرِفَهُ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Abu Sa'id al-KhudriEarliest transmitterابي سعيد الخدري
  2. 02Abu Rifa'aابي رفاعه
  3. 03Muhammad b. 'Abd al-Rahman b. Thawbanمحمد بن عبد الرحمن بن ثوبان
  4. 04Yahya b. Abi Kathirيحيي بن ابي كثير

Entry94/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 151
English
Isnad

From Mansur b. al-Mu'tamir, from Abu Ishaq al-Hamdani, from Abu al-Waddak, from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri, who said:

Matn

We took captives on the day of Hunayn and we practiced 'azl with them. We said: “The Messenger of God ﷺ is among us; shall we not ask him about this?” So we asked about that, and the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Not from all water does a child come; and whatever is decreed to be, will be.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مَنْصُورِ بْنِ الْمُعْتَمِرِ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ الْهَمْدَانِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي الْوَدَّاكِ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ، قَالَ:

Matn

أَصَبْنَا سَبَايَا يَوْمَ حُنَيْنٍ ، فَكُنَّا نَعْزِلُ عَنْهُنَّ ، فَقُلْنَا: رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ بَيْنَ أَظْهُرِنَا ، لَا نَسْأَلُهُ عَنْ هَذَا؟ فَسَأَلْنَا عَنْ ذَلِكَ ، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «لَيْسَ مِنْ كُلِّ الْمَاءِ يَكُونُ الْوَلَدُ ، وَمَا يُقدَّرُ أَنْ يَكُونَ كَانَ»

Transmission path

Entry95/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 151-152
English
Isnad

From Sa'id, from Qatada, from Abu Shaykh al-Huna'i:

Matn

that Mu'awiya b. Abi Sufyan entered a house in which were 'Abd Allah b. al-Zubayr and 'Abd Allah b. 'Amir. 'Abd Allah b. 'Amir stood up for Mu'awiya, honoring him and admiring him. Mu'awiya said to him: “Sit, for I heard the Messenger of God ﷺ say: 'Whoever likes people to stand for him, let him take his seat in the Fire.'”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي شَيْخٍ الْهُنَائِيِّ، أَنَّهُ حَدَّثَهُ،

Matn

أَنَّ مُعَاوِيَةَ بْنَ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ دَخَلَ بَيْتًا فِيهِ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ الزُّبَيْرِ وَعَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَامِرٍ ، فَقَامَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَامِرٍ لِمُعَاوِيَةَ ، فَأَعْظَمَهُ، وَيُعَجِّبُهُ ، فَقَالَ لَهُ مُعَاوِيَةُ: اجْلِسْ ، فَإِنِّي سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ يَقُولُ: «مَنْ أَحَبَّ أَنْ يَمْثُلَ لَهُ الْعِبَادُ قِيَامًا فَلْيَتَبَوَّأْ مَقْعَدَهُ مِنَ النَّارِ»

Entry96/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 152
English
Isnad

From Shu'ba, from Abu Bishr, from Sa'id b. Jubayr, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Abbas, who said:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ was taken while I had read the muhkam [explicit part] of the Qur'an and was circumcised, ten years old.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي بِشْرٍ ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ: "

Matn

قُبِضَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ وَقَدْ قَرَأْتُ مُحْكَمَ الْقُرْآنِ وَأَنَا مَخْتُونٌ ابْنُ عَشْرِ سِنِينَ

Transmission overview

1 path

English
Isnad

From Shu'ba, from Abu Ishaq, from Sa'id b. Jubayr, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Abbas, who said:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ was taken when I was fifteen years old.

العربية
Isnad

Matn

قُبِضَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ وَأَنَا ابْنُ خَمْسَ عَشْرَةَ سَنَةً»

Entry98/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 152
English
Isnad

From al-Hasan b. 'Umara, from Habib b. Abi Thabit, from Abu al-Bakhtari, that he said:

Matn

that 'Ammar b. Yasir was brought a water-skin and it was poured for him to drink, and behold, it was milk, and he laughed. He was asked: “What makes you laugh?” He said: “The Messenger of God ﷺ told me that the last drink I will drink is milk, until I die.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنِ الْحَسَنِ بْنِ عُمَارَةَ، عَنْ حَبِيبِ بْنِ أَبِي ثَابِتٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الْبَخْتَرِيِّ أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

أُتِيَ عَمَّارُ بْنُ يَاسِرٍ بِإِدَاوَةٍ ، فَصُبَّ لَهُ؛ لِيَشْرَبَ ، فَإِذَا هُوَ لَبَنٌ ، فَضَحِكَ ، فَقِيلَ لَهُ: مَا يُضْحِكُكَ؟ فَقَالَ: إِنَّ «رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ أَخْبَرَنِي: أَنَّ آخِرَ شَرَابٍ أَشْرَبُهُ لَبَنٌ حَتَّى أَمُوتَ»

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Abu al-BakhtariEarliest transmitterابي البختري
  2. 02Habib b. Abi Thabitحبيب بن ابي ثابت
  3. 03al-Hasan b. 'Umaraالحسن بن عماره
  4. 04Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

Entry99/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 152
English
Isnad

From Habib b. Abi Thabit, from Mujahid, from Abu Ma'mar, who said:

Matn

A man stood and praised one of the commanders; al-Miqdad b. al-Aswad stood up to him, threw dust in his face, and said: “The Messenger of God ﷺ ordered us to throw dust in the faces of those who praise [excessively].”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ حَبِيبِ بْنِ أَبِي ثَابِتٍ، عَنْ مُجَاهِدٍ، عَنْ أَبِي مَعْمَرٍ، قَالَ:

Matn

قَامَ رَجُلٌ فَمَدَحَ أَمِيرًا مِنَ الْأُمَرَاءِ ، فَقَامَ إِلَيْهِ الْمِقْدَادُ بْنُ الْأَسْوَدِ ، فَحَثَا فِي وَجْهِهِ التُّرَابَ ، ثُمَّ قَالَ: «أَمَرَنَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ أَنْ نَحْثِيَ فِي وجُوهِ الْمَدَّاحِينَ التُّرَابَ»

Entry100/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 152
English
Isnad

From al-Hasan b. 'Umara, from 'Atiyya b. Sa'd, from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri, who said: I heard the Messenger of God ﷺ say:

Matn

“Those on the highest levels are seen by those below them as one sees the shining star on the horizon of the sky; and Abu Bakr and 'Umar are among them, and they are excellent.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنِ الْحَسَنِ بْنِ عُمَارَةَ، عَنْ عَطِيَّةَ بْنِ سَعْدٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ يَقُولُ: «

Matn

إِنَّ أَهْلَ الدَّرَجَاتِ الْعُلَى يَرَاهُمُ الَّذِينَ أَسْفَلُ مِنْهُمْ كَمَا يُرَى الْكَوْكَبُ الدُّرِّيُّ فِي أُفُقِ السَّمَاءِ ، وَإِنَّ أَبَا بَكْرٍ وَعُمَرَ مِنْهُمْ وَأَنْعَمَا»

Entry101/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 152
English
Isnad

From al-Hasan, from al-A'mash, from Shaqiq b. Salama, from Hudhayfa b. al-Yaman, who said:

Matn

'Umar b. al-Khattab said: “Which of you remembers the hadith of the Messenger of God ﷺ about the fitna?” I said: “I.” He said: “You are bold; tell us.” I said: “I heard the Messenger of God ﷺ say: 'A man's fitna is in his family, his wealth, his children and his neighbor; prayer expiates it.'” [The hadith continues; abbreviated in the source.]

العربية
Isnad

عَنِ الْحَسَنِ، عَنِ الْأَعْمَشِ، عَنْ شَقِيقِ بْنِ سَلَمَةَ، عَنْ حُذَيْفَةَ بْنِ الْيَمَانِ، قَالَ:

Matn

قَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ: أَيُّكُمْ يَحْفَظُ حَدِيثَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ فِي الْفِتْنَةِ؟ قَالَ: قُلْتُ: أَنَا. فَقَالَ: إِنَّكَ لَجَرِيءٌ ، حَدِّثْنَا. فَقُلْتُ: سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ يَقُولُ: «تَكُونُ فِتْنَةُ الرَّجُلِ فِي أَهْلِهِ وَمَالِهِ وَوَلَدِهِ وَجَارِهِ ، تُكَفِّرُهَا الصَّلَاةُ» الْحَدِيثَ

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Hudhayfa b. al-YamanEarliest transmitterحذيفه بن اليمان
  2. 02Shaqiq b. Salamaشقيق بن سلمه
  3. 03al-A'mashالاعمش
  4. 04al-Hasanالحسن
  5. 05Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

Entry102/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 152-156
English
Isnad

From Aban, from Shahr b. Hawshab, from Ma'di Karib, from Abu Dharr, from Rabbih, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said, in what he related from his Lord: God says:

Matn

“O son of Adam, so long as you call upon Me and hope in Me, I forgive you all that is in you; even if you came to Me with an earth-load of sins, I would meet you with its like in forgiveness, provided that you associate nothing with Me; and even if you sinned until your sins reached the clouds of the sky, then asked My forgiveness, I would forgive you, and I do not mind.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ أَبَانَ، عَنْ شَهْرِ بْنِ حَوْشَبٍ، عَنْ مَعْدَِي كَرِبَ، ، عَنْ أَبِي ذَرٍّ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ فِيمَا يَذْكُرُ عَنْ رَبِّهِ: «يَقُولُ اللَّهُ:»

Matn

يَا ابْنَ آدَمَ ، إِنَّكَ مَا دَعَوْتَنِي وَرَجَوْتَنِي أَغْفِرْ لَكَ كُلَّ مَا كَانَ فِيكَ ، وَلَوْ لَقِيتَنِي بِقُرَابِ الْأَرْضِ خَطِيئَةً لَقِيتُكَ بِقُرَابِهَا مَغْفِرَةً ، بَعْدَ أَنْ لَا تُشْرِكَ بِي شَيْئًا ، وَلَوْ أَذْنَبْتَ حَتَّى تَبْلُغَ ذُنُوبُكَ عَنَانَ السَّمَاءِ ، ثُمَّ اسْتَغْفَرْتَنِي غَفَرْتُ لَكَ وَلَا أُبَالِي "

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01RabbihEarliest transmitterربه
  2. 02Abu Dharrابي ذر
  3. 03Ma'di Karibمعدي كرب
  4. 04Shahr b. Hawshabشهر بن حوشب
  5. 05Abanابان
  6. 06Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

Entry103/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 156-157
English
Isnad

From his father (not named in the text), from Abu Hurayra, from the Prophet ﷺ, who said: God Almighty says:

Matn

“I am the most self-sufficient of partners regarding association [with Me]; whoever does a deed and associates another with Me in it, I am free of him, and it belongs to the one he associated.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنِي الْعَلَاءُ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ ﷺ قَالَ: « يَقُولُ اللَّهُ عز وجل:»

Matn

أَنَا أَغْنَى الشُّرَكَاءِ عَنِ الشِّرْكِ فَمَنْ عَمِلَ عَمَلًا وَأَشْرَكَ فِيهِ غَيْرِي فَأَنَا مِنْهُ بَرِيءٌ وَهُوَ لِلَّذِي أَشْرَكَ "

  2. 02His father (not named in the text)ابيه

Entry104/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 157
English
Isnad

From his father (not named in the text), from Abu Hurayra, from the Messenger of God ﷺ, who said: God Almighty says:

Matn

“When My servant intends a good deed and does not do it, I record it for him as a good deed; if he does it, I record for him ten good deeds, up to seven hundredfold; and if My servant intends an evil deed and does not do it, I do not record it against him; if he does it, I record it for him as one evil deed.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنِي الْعَلَاءُ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ قَالَ: «يَقُولُ اللَّهُ عز وجل:»

Matn

إِذَا هَمَّ عَبْدِي بِحَسَنَةٍ وَلَمْ يَعْمَلْهَا كَتَبْتُهَا لَهُ حَسَنَةً ، فَإِنْ عَمِلَهَا: كُتِبَتْ لَهُ عَشْرُ حَسَنَاتٍ إِلَى سَبْعِ مِائَةِ ضِعْفٍ ، وَإِنْ هَمَّ عَبْدِي بِسَيِّئَةٍ فَلَمْ يَعْمَلْهَا لَمْ أَكْتُبْهَا عَلَيْهِ ، فَإِنْ عَمِلَهَا: كَتَبْتُهَا لَهُ سَيِّئَةً وَاحِدَةً "

  2. 02His father (not named in the text)ابيه

Entry105/ 208

English
Isnad

From al-'Ala', from his father (not named in the text), from Abu Hurayra, from the Messenger of God ﷺ, who said: God Almighty says:

Matn

“I asked My servant for a loan and he did not lend Me; and My servant reviled Me, and it was not fitting for him to revile Me, saying: Alas, time! – and I am Time.” Three times.

العربية
Isnad

عَنِ الْعَلَاءِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ قَالَ: «يَقُولُ اللَّهُ عز وجل:»

Matn

اسْتَقْرَضْتُ عَبْدِي فَلَمْ يُقْرِضْنِي ، وَشَتَمَنِي عَبْدِي وَلَمْ يَنْبَغِي لَهُ شَتْمِي ، يَقُولُ: وَادَهْرَاهُ وَأَنَا الدَّهْرُ ". ثَلَاثًا

Entry107/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 157-159
English
Isnad

From Hisham, from Muhammad b. Sirin, from Abu Hurayra, from the Messenger of God ﷺ, who said: God Almighty says:

Matn

“A good deed is ten times its like; and fasting is Mine and I give recompense for it: he leaves his food and drink for My sake; and the odor of the fasting person's mouth is sweeter to God than the scent of musk.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ هِشَامٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ سِيرِينَ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ قَالَ: «يَقُولُ اللَّهُ عز وجل:»

Matn

الْحَسَنَةُ بِعَشْرِ أَمْثَالِهَا ، وَالصَّوْمُ لِي ، وَأَنَا أَجْزِي بِهِ ، إِنَّهُ يَذَرُ طَعَامَهُ وَشَرَابَهُ مِنْ أَجْلِي ، وَلَخُلُوفُ فَمِ الصَّائِمِ أَطْيَبُ عِنْدَ اللَّهِ مِنْ رِيحِ الْمِسْكِ "

Matn

Matn

Isnad

From Layth b. Abi Sulaym, from 'Uthman, from Anas b. Malik, who said: I heard the Messenger of God ﷺ say:

Matn

“Jibril came to me with something like a white mirror in his palm, on which was something like a black spot.” And he mentioned the hadith of Friday.

Matn

English
Isnad

From Ayyub (al-Sakhtiyani), from Muhammad b. Sirin, from Some of the sons of Abu Bakra (not named), from Abu Bakra, who said:

Matn

I heard the Messenger of God ﷺ say on the Day of Sacrifice: “Time has come round to its form on the day God created the heavens and the earth; the year is twelve months.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ أَيُّوبَ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ سِيرِينَ، عَنْ بَعْضِ بَنِي أَبِي بَكْرَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي بَكْرَةَ، قَالَ:

Matn

سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ يَقُولُ يَوْمَ النَّحْرِ: «إِنَّ الزَّمَانَ قَدِ اسْتَدَارَ كَهَيْئَتِهِ يَوْمَ خَلَقَ اللَّهُ السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضَ ، السَّنَةُ اثْنَا عَشَرَ شَهْرًا»

English
Isnad

From Muhammad b. Ziyad, from Abu Hurayra, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said: Your Lord said:

Matn

“Every deed is an expiation; and fasting is Mine and I give recompense for it; and the odor of the fasting person's mouth is sweeter to God than the scent of musk.”

العربية
Isnad

حَدَّثَنِي إِبْرَاهِيمُ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ زِيَادٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «قَالَ رَبُّكُمْ:»

Matn

كُلُّ الْعَمَلِ كَفَّارَةٌ ، وَالصَّوْمُ لِي وَأَنَا أَجْزِي بِهِ ، وَلَخُلُوفُ فَمِ الصَّائِمِ أَطْيَبُ عِنْدَ اللَّهِ مِنْ رِيحِ الْمِسْكِ "

Entry117/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 166-167
English
Isnad

From Muhammad b. Ziyad, from Abu Hurayra:

Matn

that he saw a man dragging his lower garment, and shouted at him, then said to him: “Have you not heard what Abu al-Qasim ﷺ said? He said: 'God Almighty does not look at one who drags his lower garment (or he said: his garments) out of insolence.'”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ زِيَادٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ،

Matn

أَنَّهُ رَأَى رَجُلًا يَجُرُّ إِزَارَهُ ، فَصَاحَ بِهِ ، ثُمَّ قَالَ لَهُ: أَوَمَا سَمِعْتَ مَا قَالَ أَبُو الْقَاسِمِ ﷺ قَالَ: «إِنَّ اللَّهَ عز وجل لَا يَنْظُرُ إِلَى مَنْ يَجُرُّ إِزَارَهُ، أَوْ قَالَ ثِيَابَهُ، بَطَرًا»

Isnad

From Muhammad b. Abi Dhi'b, from al-Zuhri, from al-A'raj, from Abu Hurayra, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The sun has not risen on a day better than Friday: in it Adam was created, in it he entered Paradise, and in it he was brought out of it.”

Matn

English
Isnad

From Shu'ba, from Qatada, from Anas b. Malik, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The Lote Tree of the Utmost Boundary was raised for me, and there were four rivers: two outward and two inward. As for the outward ones, they are the Nile and the Euphrates; and as for the inward ones, they are two rivers in Paradise.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ «

Matn

رُفِعَتْ إِلَيَّ سِدْرَةُ الْمُنْتَهَى ، فَإِذَا أَرْبَعَةُ أَنْهَارٍ: نَهْرَانِ ظَاهِرَانِ ، وَنَهْرَانِ بَاطِنَانِ ، فَأَمَّا الظَّاهِرَانِ فَالنِّيلُ وَالْفُرَاتُ ، وَأَمَّا الْبَاطِنَانِ فَنَهَرَانِ فِي الْجَنَّةِ»

Entry120/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 167-169
English
Isnad

From Shu'ba, from al-'Ala', from his father (not named in the text), from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri:

Matn

that he asked him about the lower garment, and he said: “You have fallen upon one who knows. I heard the Messenger of God ﷺ say: 'The believer's lower garment is to the middle of the shins.'”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، عَنِ الْعَلَاءِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ،

Matn

أَنَّهُ سَأَلَهُ عَنِ الْإِزَارِ، فَقَالَ: عَلَى الْخَبِيرِ سَقَطْتَ ، سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ يَقُولُ: «إِزْرَةُ الْمُؤْمِنِ إِلَى أَنْصَافِ السَّاقَيْنِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Entry121/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 169
English
Isnad

From Shu'ba, from Qatada, from Anas b. Malik, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“When one of you is in his prayer he is conversing with his Lord, so let him not spit to his right or in front of him, but to his left under his feet.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

إِذَا كَانَ أَحَدُكُمْ فِي صَلَاتِهِ فَإِنَّهُ يُنَاجِي رَبَّهُ ، فَلَا يَتْفُلَنَّ عَنْ يَمِينِهِ وَلَا بَيْنَ يَدَيْهِ، وَلَكِنْ عَنْ يَسَارِهِ تَحْتَ قَدَمَيْهِ»

Entry122/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 169
English
Isnad

From 'Umar b. Sa'id al-Kufi, from Sulayman, from Abu Hazim, from Abu Hurayra, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Three whom God will not speak to, nor look at on the Day of Resurrection, nor purify, and for them is a painful punishment: a lying king, a proud poor man, and an adulterous old man.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ عُمَرَ بْنِ سَعِيدٍ الْكُوفِيِّ ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ، عَنْ أَبِي حَازِمٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

ثَلَاثَةٌ لَا يُكَلِّمُهُمُ اللَّهُ ، وَلَا يَنْظُرُ إِلَيْهِمْ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ ، وَلَا يُزَكِّيهِمْ ، وَلَهُمْ عَذَابٌ أَلِيمٌ: مَلِكٌ كَذَّابٌ ، وَعَائِلٌ مُسْتَكْبِرٌ ، وَشَيْخٌ زَانٍ»

Entry123/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 169
English
Isnad

From Sufyan b. Sa'id (al-Thawri), from Musa b. Abi Ja'far, from Salim b. Abi al-Ja'd, from Kurayb, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Abbas, who said:

Matn

A man of the Banu Sa'd b. Bakr came to the Messenger of God ﷺ – and the Messenger of God ﷺ had been nursed among them – and said: “O son of 'Abd al-Muttalib, I am a man of your maternal uncles, and I am the messenger of my people and their envoy to you; I will question you and press my questions, and I adjure you, so my adjuration will be intense; so do not be angry with me.” He said: “Yes.” He said: “Tell me: who created you, and created those before you and those after you?” He said: “God.” [The hadith continues; abbreviated in the source.]

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ سُفْيَانَ بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ أَبِي جَعْفَرٍ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ أَبِي الْجَعْدِ، عَنْ كُرَيْبٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ:

Matn

جَاءَ رَجُلٌ مِنْ بَنِي سَعْدِ بْنِ بَكْرٍ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ قَالَ: وَكَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ مُسْتَرْضَعًا فِيهِمْ، فَقَالَ: يَا ابْنَ عَبْدِ الْمُطَّلِبِ، أَنَا رَجُلٌ مِنْ أَخْوَالِكَ، وَأَنَا رَسُولُ قَوْمِي، وَوَافِدُهُمْ إِلَيْكَ، وَأَنَا سَائِلُكَ وَمُشَدِّدٌ سُؤَالِي إِيَّاكَ، وَمُنْشِدُكَ فَمُشْتَدٌّ إِنْشَادِي إِيَّاكَ، فَلَا تَجِدَنَّ عَلَيَّ قَالَ: «نَعَمْ» . قَالَ: أَخْبِرْنِي «مَنْ خَالِقُكَ، وَخَالِقُ مَنْ قَبْلَكَ، وَخَالِقُ مَنْ بَعْدَكَ؟ قَالَ:» اللَّهُ " الْحَدِيثَ

  2. 02Kuraybكريب
  3. 03Salim b. Abi al-Ja'dسالم بن ابي الجعد

Entry124/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 169
English
Isnad

From 'Ubayd Allah b. 'Umar, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said:

Matn

While the Messenger of God ﷺ was standing leading the people in prayer, he saw phlegm on the qibla wall of the mosque and scraped it off; then he forbade people to spit in front of their faces when in prayer, and said: “God is before the face of each of you when he is in prayer, so let none of you spit before his face when in prayer.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ:

Matn

بَيْنَمَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ قَائِمٌ يُصَلِّي بِالنَّاسِ، فَرَأَى نُخَامَةً فِي قِبْلَةِ الْمَسْجِدِ فَحَتَّهَا، ثُمَّ نَهَى النَّاسَ أَنْ يَتَنَخَّمَ أَحَدُهُمْ إِذَا كَانَ فِي الصَّلَاةِ قِبَلَ وَجْهِهِ، فَقَالَ: إِنَّ «اللَّهَ قِبَلُ وَجْهِ أَحَدِكُمْ إِذَا كَانَ فِي الصَّلَاةِ، فَلَا يَتَنَخَّمْ أَحَدُكُمْ قِبَلَ وَجْهِهِ إِذَا كَانَ فِي الصَّلَاةِ»

Transmission path

  2. 02Nafi'نافع
  3. 03'Ubayd Allah b. 'Umarعبيد الله بن عمر

Entry125/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 169-173
English
Isnad

From 'Asim b. Abi al-Najud, from Zirr b. Hubaysh, from Ubayy b. Ka'b, who said:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ met Jibril at Ahjar al-Mira' and said to him: “You were sent to an unlettered community, among whom are the young, the decrepit old man and the old woman; so let them recite the Qur'an in seven modes (ahruf).”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ عَاصِمِ بْنِ أَبِي النَّجُودِ، عَنْ زِرِّ بْنِ حُبَيْشٍ، عَنْ أُبَيِّ بْنِ كَعْبٍ، قَالَ:

Matn

لَقِيَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ جِبْرِيلَ عِنْدَ أَحْجَارِ الْمَرَائِي، فَقَالَ لَهُ: «إِنَّكَ بُعِثْتَ إِلَى أُمَّةٍ أُمِّيِّينَ، فِيهِمُ الصَّغِيرُ، وَالشَّيْخُ الْفَانِي، وَالْعَجُوزُ، فَلْيَقْرَءُوا الْقُرْآنَ عَلَى سَبْعَةِ أَحْرُفٍ»

Transmission path

  1. 01Ubayy b. Ka'bEarliest transmitterابي بن كعب
  2. 02Zirr b. Hubayshزر بن حبيش
  3. 03'Asim b. Abi al-Najudعاصم بن ابي النجود
  4. 04Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

Entry127/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 173-176
English
Isnad

From al-Hasan b. 'Umara, from al-A'mash, from Abu Salih, from Abu Hurayra, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said: God Almighty says:

Matn

“My servant is as he thinks of Me, and I am with him when he remembers Me. If he remembers Me within himself, I remember him within Myself; if he remembers Me alone, I remember him alone; if he remembers Me in a gathering, I remember him in a gathering better than theirs; if he draws near to Me by a span, I draw near to him by a cubit; if he draws near to Me by a cubit, I draw near to him by an arm's length; and if he comes to Me walking, I come to him hastening.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنِ الْحَسَنِ بْنِ عُمَارَةَ، عَنِ الْأَعْمَشِ، عَنْ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «يَقُولُ اللَّهُ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ:»

Matn

عَبْدِي عِنْدَ ظَنِّهِ بِي، وَأَنَا مَعَهُ حِينَ يَذْكُرُنِي، إِنْ ذَكَرَنِيَ فِي نَفْسِهِ ذَكَرْتُهُ فِي نَفْسِي، وَإِنْ ذَكَرَنِي وَحْدَهُ ذَكَرْتُهُ وَحْدِي، وَإِنْ ذَكَرَنِي فِي مَلَإٍ ذَكَرْتُهُ فِي مَلَإٍ خَيْرٍ مِنْهُمْ، وَإِنْ تَقَرَّبَ إِلَيَّ شِبْرًا تَقَرَّبْتُ إِلَيْهِ ذِرَاعًا، وَإِنْ تَقَرَّبَ إِلَيَّ ذِرَاعًا تَقَرَّبْتُ مِنْهُ بَاعًا، وَإِنْ أَتَانِي يَمْشِي أَتَيْتُهُ هَرْوَلَةً "

  2. 02Abu Salihابي صالح

English
Isnad

From al-Hajjaj, from Ayyub b. Abi Tamima, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

Matn

صَلَاةُ الْجَمَاعَةِ تَفْضُلُ صَلَاةَ الْفَذِّ بِسَبْعٍ وَعِشْرِينَ دَرَجَةً»

Entry129/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 177-178
English
Isnad

From Husayn, from Yahya b. Abi Kathir, from Muhammad b. 'Abd al-Rahman, from 'Abbad b. Aws, from Abu Hurayra, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Every step one of you takes toward prayer is recorded for him as a good deed and wipes out a bad deed from him.” He said, or he said: “Is recorded for him as a good deed or wipes out a bad deed from him.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ حُسَيْنٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ أَبِي كَثِيرٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ عَبَّادِ بْنِ أَوْسٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

كُلُّ خُطْوَةٍ يَخْطُوهَا أَحَدُكُمْ إِلَى الصَّلَاةِ تُكْتَبُ لَهُ حَسَنَةٌ، وَتُمْحَى عَنْهُ بِهَا سَيِّئَةٌ قَالَ أَوْ قَالَ:» تُكْتَبُ لَهُ حَسَنَةٌ أَوْ تُمْحَى عَنْهُ بِهَا سَيِّئَةٌ "

Entry131/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 178
English
Isnad

By the same chain as report 130: from Muhammad b. Ziyad, from Abu Hurayra, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“A woman entered the Fire because of a cat of hers: she bound it, neither feeding it nor letting it eat of the vermin of the earth, until it died of emaciation.”

العربية
Isnad

وَبِهِ قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

دَخَلَتِ امْرَأَةٌ النَّارَ مِنْ أَجْلِ هِرَّةٍ لَهَا؛ أَوْثَقَتْهَا، فَلَا هِيَ أَطْعَمَتْهَا، وَلَا هِيَ تَرَكَتْهَا تَأْكُلُ مِنْ خَشَاشِ الْأَرْضِ حَتَّى مَاتَتْ هَزْلًا»

Entry132/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 178-181
English
Isnad

From 'Abbad b. Ishaq, from Abu al-Zinad, from Yazid al-Raqashi, from Anas b. Malik, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“God remains in the need of [helping] one of you as long as he is engaged in the need of his brother; and any person who loosens from another a ring [of hardship], by it his need will be met on the Day of Resurrection.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ عَبَّادِ بْنِ إِسْحَاقَ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ الرَّقَاشِيِّ، عَنْ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

لَا يَزَالُ اللَّهُ فِي حَاجَةِ أَحَدِكُمْ مَا كَانَ فِي حَاجَةِ أَخِيهِ، وَإِنَّمَا امْرُؤٌ فَكَّ عَنِ امْرِئٍ حَلْقَةً بِهَا عَنْهُ حَاجَتَهُ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ»

Entry133/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 181
English
Isnad

From Malik b. Anas, from Muhammad b. 'Ajlan, from his father (not named in the text), from Abu Hurayra, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The slave has his food and his clothing in a fair manner, and is not to be burdened with work he cannot bear.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ أَنَسٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَجْلَانَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

لِلْمَمْلُوكِ طَعَامُهُ وَكِسْوَتُهُ بِالْمَعْرُوفِ، وَلَا يُكَلَّفُ مِنَ الْعَمَلِ مَا لَا يُطِيقُ»

Entry134/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 181-182
English
Isnad

From Yahya b. Sa'id, who said: I heard 'Abd Allah b. 'Amir say: 'A'isha said:

Matn

I saw the Messenger of God ﷺ sleepless one night, and he said: “If only a righteous man would guard me tonight.” She said: Then I heard the sound of weapons, and the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Who is this?” He said: “Sa'd b. Abi Waqqas, O Messenger of God; I came to guard you tonight.” She said: The Messenger of God ﷺ then slept until I heard his snoring.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَامِرٍ، يَقُولُ: قَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ:

Matn

رَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ أَرِقَ ذَاتَ لَيْلَةٍ، فَقَالَ: «لَيْتَ رَجُلًا صَالِحًا يَحْرُسُنِي اللَّيْلَةَ» . قَالَتْ: إِذْ سَمِعْتُ صَوْتَ السِّلَاحِ، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «مَنْ هَذَا؟» فَقَالَ: سَعْدُ بْنُ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، جِئْتُ أَحْرُسُكَ اللَّيْلَةَ. قَالَتْ: فَنَامَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ حَتَّى سَمِعْتُ غَطِيطَهُ

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Yahya b. Sa'idEarliest transmitterيحيي بن سعيد
  2. 02Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

Entry135/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 182
English
Isnad

From Yahya b. Sa'id, from Ya'qub b. Khalid, from Abu Salih al-Samman, from Zayd b. Khalid al-Juhani, as far as I know, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Quraysh, the Ansar, Aslam, Ghifar, and those of Ashja' and Juhayna – or Juhayna and Ashja' – are allies and clients; they have no master besides God and His Messenger.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ يَعْقُوبَ بْنِ خَالِدٍ، عَنْ أَبِي صَالِحٍ السَّمَّانِ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ خَالِدٍ الْجُهَنِيِّ، فِيمَا أَعْلَمُ قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

قُرَيْشٌ، وَالْأَنْصَارُ، وَأَسْلَمُ، وَغِفَارٌ، وَمَنْ كَانَ مِنْ أَشْجَعَ، وَجُهَيْنَةَ، أَوْ جُهَيْنَةَ، وَأَشْجَعَ حُلَفَاءٌ مَوَالِي، لَيْسَ لَهُمْ دُونَ اللَّهِ وَرَسُولِهِ مَوْلًى»

Entry136/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 182
English
Isnad

From Musa b. 'Uqba, from Salim, from his father (not named in the text), who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“I saw as though the people had gathered, and Abu Bakr rose and drew a bucket, and in his drawing there was weakness; then 'Umar rose and it turned into a large bucket, and I saw no genius among the people doing such work as he did, until the people watered their camels at the watering place.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

رَأَيْتُ كَأَنَّ النَّاسَ اجْتَمَعُوا، فَقَامَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ فَنَزَعَ ذَنُوبًا، وَفِي نَزْعِهِ ضَعْفٌ، ثُمَّ قَامَ عُمَرُ فَاسْتَحَالَتْ غَرْبًا فَمَا رَأَيْتُ عَبْقَرِيًّا مِنَ النَّاسِ يَفْرِي فَرِيَّهُ حَتَّى ضَرَبَ النَّاسَ بِعَطَنٍ»

Entry137/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 182-185
English
Isnad

From Malik b. Anas, from Abu al-Nadr, mawla of 'Umar b. 'Ubayd Allah, from 'Ubayd b. Hunayn, from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri, who said:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ sat and said: “A servant was given a choice by his Lord between being given of the flowers of this world whatever he wished and what is with Him, and he chose what is with Him.” Abu Bakr wept and said: “We would ransom you with our fathers and mothers.” He said: We marveled at him, and the people said: “Look at this old man: the Messenger of God ﷺ tells of a servant whom his Lord gave a choice, and this man says: We would ransom you with our fathers and mothers!” But the Messenger of God ﷺ was the one given the choice, and Abu Bakr was the most knowing of us about it. The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “The most generous of people to me in his companionship and his wealth is Abu Bakr; and if I were to take a close friend (khalil), I would take Abu Bakr, but [there is] the brotherhood of Islam.” Then he said: “Let no door remain open in the mosque except the door of Abu Bakr.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ أَنَسٍ، عَنْ أَبِي النَّضْرِ، مَوْلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ بْنِ حُنَيْنٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، قَالَ:

Matn

جَلَسَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ فَقَالَ: «إِنَّ عَبْدًا خَيَّرَهُ رَبُّهُ بَيْنَ أَنْ يُؤْتِيَهُ مِنْ زَهْرَةِ الدُّنْيَا مَا شَاءَ وَبَيْنَ مَا عِنْدَهُ، فَاخْتَارَ مَا عِنْدَهُ، فَبَكَى أَبُو بَكْرٍ، ثُمَّ قَالَ: فَدَيْنَاكَ بِآبَائِنَا وَأُمَّهَاتِنَا. قَالَ: فَعَجِبْنَا لَهُ فَقَالَ النَّاسُ: انْظُرُوا إِلَى هَذَا الشَّيْخِ يُخْبِرُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ عَنْ عَبْدٍ خَيَّرَهُ رَبُّهُ، وَهَذَا يَقُولُ: فَدَيْنَاكَ بِآبَائِنَا وَأُمَّهَاتِنَا فَكَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ هُوَ الْمُخَيَّرُ، وَكَانَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ أَعْلَمَنَا بِهِ، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ:» إِنَّ أَمَنَّ النَّاسِ عَلَيَّ فِي صُحْبَتِهِ وَمَالِهِ أَبُو بَكْرٍ، وَلَوْ كُنْتُ مُتَّخِذًا خَلِيلًا لَاتَّخَذْتُ أَبَا بَكْرٍ، وَلَكِنْ أُخُوَّةُ الْإِسْلَامِ «. ثُمَّ قَالَ:» لَا يَبْقِيَنَّ فِي الْمَسْجِدِ إِلَّا خَوْخَةُ أَبِي بَكْرٍ "

Entry138/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 185-186
English
Isnad

From Musa b. 'Uqba, from Salim, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, from the Messenger of God ﷺ, who said:

Matn

when the Messenger of God ﷺ appointed Usama b. Zayd, it reached him that the people criticized his command and found fault with it. The Messenger of God ﷺ stood and said: “Indeed you criticize Usama and find fault with his command, and you did the same to his father before; he was fit for command, and he was among the most beloved of all people to me, and his son is among the most beloved of people to me. So treat him well, for he is one of your best.” Salim said: I never heard 'Abd Allah [b. 'Umar] narrate this hadith except that he said: “By God, he did not except Fatima.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ قَالَ:

Matn

حِينَ أَمَّرَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ أُسَامَةَ بْنَ زَيْدٍ، فَبَلَغَهُ أَنَّ النَّاسَ عَابُوا إِمَارَتَهُ، فَطَعَنُوا فِيهَا، فَقَامَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ فَقَالَ: « أَلَا إِنَّكُمْ تَعِيبُونَ أُسَامَةَ، وَتَطْعَنُونَ فِي إِمَارَتِهِ، وَقَدْ فَعَلْتُمْ ذَلِكَ بِأَبِيهِ مِنْ قَبْلُ، وَإِنْ كَانَ لَخَلِيقًا بِالْإِمَارَةِ، وَإِنْ كَانَ لَأَحَبَّ النَّاسِ كُلِّهِمْ إِلَيَّ، وَإِنَّ ابْنَهُ لَأَحَبُّ النَّاسِ إِلَيَّ، فَاسْتَوْصُوا بِهِ خَيْرًا، فَإِنَّهُ مِنْ خِيَارِكُمْ» فَقَالَ سَالِمٌ: مَا سَمِعْتُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ يُحَدِّثُ بِهَذَا الْحَدِيثِ قَطُّ إِلَّا قَالَ: وَاللَّهِ مَا حَاشَا فَاطِمَةَ

  2. 02Salimسالم
  3. 03Musa b. 'Uqbaموسي بن عقبه

Entry139/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 186-187
English
Isnad

From Yahya b. Sa'id, from Sa'id, who said: I heard Sa'd say:

Matn

“The Qur'an was sent down to the Messenger of God ﷺ when he was forty-three years old; he stayed in Mecca ten years and in Medina ten years, and was taken when he was sixty-three.” He said: Abu Bakr completed in his caliphate the age of the Messenger of God ﷺ; he died at the age of the Messenger of God ﷺ.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدٍ، قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ سَعْدًا، يَقُولُ: «

Matn

أُنْزِلَ عَلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ الْقُرْآنُ وَهُوَ ابْنُ ثَلَاثٍ وَأَرْبَعِينَ سَنَةً، فَأَقَامَ بِمَكَّةَ عَشْرَ سِنِينَ، وَبِالْمَدِينَةِ عَشْرَ سِنِينَ، فَقُبِضَ وَهُوَ ابْنُ ثَلَاثٍ وَسِتِّينَ. فَقَالَ: اسْتَكْمَلَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ فِي خِلَافَتِهِ سِنَّ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ، فَتُوُفِّيَ وَهُوَ فِي سِنِّ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ»

Transmission overview

Entry142/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 187
English
Isnad

From al-Hasan b. 'Umara, from al-A'mash, from Musa b. 'Abd Allah b. Yazid, from 'Abd al-Rahman b. Hilal, from Jarir b. 'Abd Allah, from the Messenger of God ﷺ, who said:

Matn

“The Emigrants and the Ansar are allies of one another in this world and the next; and the freed ones (tulaqa') of Quraysh and the emancipated ones (utaqa') of Thaqif are allies of one another.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنِ الْحَسَنِ بْنِ عُمَارَةَ، عَنِ الْأَعْمَشِ، عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ هِلَالٍ، عَنْ جَرِيرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ قَالَ: «

Matn

الْمُهَاجِرُونَ، وَالْأَنْصَارُ بَعْضُهُمْ أَوْلِيَاءُ بَعْضٍ فِي الدُّنْيَا وَالْآخِرَةِ، وَالطُّلَقَاءُ مِنْ قُرَيْشٍ، وَالْعُتَقَاءُ مِنْ ثَقِيفٍ بَعْضُهُمْ أَوْلِيَاءُ بَعْضٍ»

Entry143/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 187-190
English
Isnad

From al-Hasan b. 'Umara, from al-A'mash, from Shaqiq b. Salama, that he said:

Matn

'Abd al-Rahman b. 'Awf entered upon Umm Salama and said: “O Mother, I fear that I have perished; I am the richest of Quraysh in wealth, and I sold land of mine for forty thousand dinars.” She said: “Spend, my son, for I heard the Messenger of God ﷺ say: 'Among my companions are those who will not see me after I part from them, ever.'”

العربية
Isnad

وَبِهِ عَنِ الْأَعْمَشِ، عَنْ شَقِيقِ بْنِ سَلَمَةَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

دَخَلَ عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَوْفٍ عَلَى أُمِّ سَلَمَةَ فَقَالَ: يَا أُمَّتَاهْ، إِنِّي قَدْ خَشِيتُ أَنْ أَكُونَ قَدْ هَلَكْتُ، أَنَا أَكْثَرُ قُرَيْشٍ مَالًا، وَقَدْ بِعْتُ أَرْضًا لِي بِأَرْبَعِينَ أَلْفَ دِينَارٍ؟ فَقَالَتْ: أَنْفِقْ يَا بُنَيَّ، فَإِنِّي سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ يَقُولُ: «إِنَّ مِنْ أَصْحَابِي مَنْ لَا يَرَانِي بَعْدَ أَنْ أُفَارِقَهُ أَبَدًا»

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Shaqiq b. SalamaEarliest transmitterشقيق بن سلمة
  2. 02al-A'mashالأعمش
  3. 03al-Hasan b. 'Umaraالحسن بن عمارة

Isnad

Entry145/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 190
English
Isnad

From al-Hasan b. 'Umara, from al-A'mash, from Abu Salih, from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri, who said:

Matn

Khalid b. al-Walid and 'Abd al-Rahman b. 'Awf reviled one another [text reads: insab], and the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Do not revile my Companions; for if one of you spent the like of Uhud in gold, he would not reach a mudd of one of them, nor half of it.”

العربية
Isnad

وَبِهِ عَنِ الْأَعْمَشِ، عَنْ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ، قَالَ:

Matn

انْسَبَّ خَالِدُ بْنُ الْوَلِيدِ وَعَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ عَوْفٍ، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «لَا تَسُبُّوا أَصْحَابِي، فَإِنَّ أَحَدَكُمْ لَوْ أَنْفَقَ مِثْلَ أُحُدٍ ذَهَبًا مَا أَدْرَكَ مُدَّ أَحَدِهِمْ وَلَا نَصِيفَهُ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Abu Sa'id al-KhudriEarliest transmitterأبي سعيد
  2. 02Abu Salihأبي صالح
  3. 03al-A'mashالأعمش
  4. 04al-Hasan b. 'Umaraالحسن بن عمارة
  5. 05Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

Entry146/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 190
English
Isnad

From al-Hasan b. 'Umara, from al-Hakam, from Miqsam, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Abbas, who said:

Matn

Haritha b. al-Nu'man passed by the Messenger of God ﷺ while Jibril was with him. Jibril said: “O Messenger of God, this is Haritha b. al-Nu'man.” He said: “Yes.” Jibril said: “He is one of the eighty who stood firm with the Messenger of God ﷺ on the day of Hunayn; God has guaranteed their provisions and the provisions of their families in Paradise.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنِ الْحَسَنِ بْنِ عُمَارَةَ، عَنِ الْحَكَمِ، عَنْ مِقْسَمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ:

Matn

مَرَّ حَارِثَةُ بْنُ النُّعْمَانِ عَلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ وَعِنْدَهُ جِبْرِيلُ، فَقَالَ جِبْرِيلُ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، هَذَا حَارِثَةُ بْنُ النُّعْمَانِ. قَالَ: «نَعَمْ» . قَالَ جِبْرِيلُ: «أَمَا أَنَّهُ أَحَدُ الثَّمَانِينَ الَّذِينَ ثَبَتُوا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ يَوْمَ حُنَيْنٍ، تَكَفَّلَ اللَّهُ بِأَرْزَاقِهِمْ وَأَرْزَاقِ عِيَالَاتِهِمْ فِي الْجَنَّةِ»

Transmission path

  1. 01'Abd Allah b. 'AbbasEarliest transmitterابن عباس
  2. 02Miqsamمقسم
  3. 03al-Hakamالحكم

Entry147/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 190-193
English
Isnad

From 'Abbad b. Ishaq, from Muhammad b. 'Abd Allah b. Muslim, from Muhammad b. Muslim b. Shihab (al-Zuhri), from Sa'id b. al-Musayyab, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“While I was asleep I was brought a cup of milk and drank until I saw the quenching come out of my fingernails; then I handed my remainder to 'Umar and he drank.” It was said: “How did you interpret that, O Messenger of God?” He said: “Knowledge.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ عَبَّادِ بْنِ إِسْحَاقَ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللِّهِ بْنِ مُسْلِمٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ مُسْلِمِ بْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، وَحَمْزَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

بَيْنَا أَنَا نَائِمٌ أُوتِيتُ بِقَدَحٍ فِيهِ لَبَنٌ، فَشَرِبْتُ حَتَّى رَأَيْتُ الرِّيَّ يَخْرُجُ مِنْ أَظَافِيرِي، ثُمَّ نَاوَلْتُ فَضْلِي عُمَرَ فَشَرِبَ» . فَقِيلَ: مَا أَوَّلْتَ ذَلِكَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ؟ قَالَ: «الْعِلْمُ»

  2. 02Sa'id b. al-Musayyabسعيد بن المسيب
  3. 03Muhammad b. Muslim b. Shihab (al-Zuhri)محمد بن مسلم بن شهاب
  4. 04Muhammad b. 'Abd Allah b. Muslimمحمد بن عبد الله بن مسلم
  5. 05'Abbad b. Ishaqعباد بن إسحاق
  6. 06Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

  2. 02Hamza b. 'Abd Allah b. 'Umarحمزة بن عبد الله بن عمر

Entry148/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 193-194
English
Isnad

From 'Abbad b. Ishaq, from Muhammad b. 'Abd Allah b. Muslim, from Muhammad b. Muslim b. Shihab (al-Zuhri), from Ibn al-Musayyab, from Abu Hurayra, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“While I was asleep I saw myself in Paradise, and there was a palace built, beside which was a girl. I said: 'Whose is this palace?' She said: 'It is 'Umar's.' So I turned away, knowing his jealousy.” And 'Umar was sitting, until he narrated this. He said: He wept, and said: “May my father be your ransom, O Messenger of God, would I be jealous of you?”

العربية
Isnad

وَبِهِ عَنِ ابْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

بَيْنَا أَنَا نَائِمٌ إِذْ رَأَيْتُ أَنِّي فِيَ الْجَنَّةِ، فَإِذَا قَصْرٌ مَبْنِيٌّ، إِلَى جَانِبِهِ جَارِيَةٌ، فَقُلْتُ: لِمَنْ هَذَا الْقَصْرُ؟ قَالَتْ: لِعُمَرَ. فَوَلَّيْتُ مُدْبِرًا لِعِلْمِي غَيْرَتَهُ» . وَعُمَرُ جَالِسٌ حَتَّى يُحَدِّثَ بِهَذَا، قَالَ: فَبَكَى، فَقَالَ: بِأَبِي أَنْتَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ أَعَلَيْكَ أَغَارُ

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Abu HurayraEarliest transmitterأبي هريرة
  2. 02Ibn al-Musayyabابن المسيب
  3. 03Muhammad b. Muslim b. Shihab (al-Zuhri)محمد بن مسلم بن شهاب
  4. 04Muhammad b. 'Abd Allah b. Muslimمحمد بن عبد الله بن مسلم
  5. 05'Abbad b. Ishaqعباد بن إسحاق

Entry149/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 194
English
Isnad

From 'Abbad b. Ishaq, from Muhammad b. 'Abd Allah b. Muslim, from Muhammad b. Muslim b. Shihab (al-Zuhri), from Abu Umama b. Sahl, from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“While I was asleep I saw people being presented, wearing shirts, some to their hems and some below that; 'Umar was presented, wearing a shirt that he dragged.” It was said: “How did you interpret that, O Messenger of God?” He said: “Religion (din).”

العربية
Isnad

وَبِهِ عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ أَبِي أُمَامَةَ بْنِ سَهْلٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

بَيْنَا أَنَا نَائِمٌ رَأَيْتُ كَأَنَّ النَّاسَ يُعْرَضُونَ، عَلَيْهِمْ قُمُصٌ إِلَى ذَيْلِهِمْ وَأَسْفَلَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ، فَعُرِضَ عَلَيَّ عُمَرُ، عَلَيْهِ قَمِيصٌ يَجُرُّهُ» . قِيلَ: مَا أَوَّلْتَ ذَلِكَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ؟ قَالَ: «الدِّينُ»

1 path

Transmission path

  2. 02Abu Umama b. Sahlأبي أمامة بن سهل
  3. 03Muhammad b. Muslim b. Shihab (al-Zuhri)محمد بن مسلم بن شهاب
  4. 04Muhammad b. 'Abd Allah b. Muslimمحمد بن عبد الله بن مسلم

Entry150/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 194
English
Isnad

From Muhammad b. Abi Dhi'b, from Ibn Shihab (al-Zuhri), from Yahya b. Sa'id b. al-'As, from his father (not named in the text), from 'Uthman b. 'Affan:

Matn

that Abu Bakr asked permission [to enter] while the Messenger of God ﷺ was on his bed wearing 'A'isha's wrapper; he permitted him, and he spoke to him about his need, then left; then 'Umar came. [The hadith continues; abbreviated in the source.]

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ أَبِي ذِئْبٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْعَاصِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عُثْمَانَ بْنِ عَفَّانَ، وَعَائِشَةَ أَنَّهُمَا حَدَّثَاهُ:

Matn

أَنَّ أَبَا بَكْرٍ اسْتَأْذَنَ وَرَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ " عَلَى فِرَاشِهِ لَابِسٌ مِرْطَ عَائِشَةَ، فَأَذِنَ لَهُ، فَكَلَّمَهُ بِحَاجَتِهِ، ثُمَّ خَرَجَ، ثُمَّ جَاءَ عُمَرُ الْحَدِيثَ

Isnad

From al-Hajjaj b. al-Hajjaj, from 'Abd al-Rahman b. al-Qasim, from his father (not named in the text), from 'A'isha, who said:

Matn

I used to twist the garlands for the sacrificial camels of the Messenger of God ﷺ; he would send them [to Mecca], and then would not refrain from anything that was lawful for him.

العربية

Matn

Entry154/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 196
English
Isnad

From 'Abbad b. Ishaq, from al-Zuhri, from Salim, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The prayer of the traveler is two rak'as.” Abu Bakr prayed it two rak'as, 'Umar prayed it two rak'as, and 'Uthman prayed it two rak'as in the early part of his caliphate, then completed it afterwards.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ عَبَّادِ بْنِ إِسْحَاقَ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

صَلَاةُ الْمُسَافِرِ رَكْعَتَانِ» وَصَلَّاهَا أَبُو بَكْرٍ رَكْعَتَيْنِ، وَصَلَّاهَا عُمَرُ رَكْعَتَيْنِ، وَصَلَّاهَا عُثْمَانُ رَكْعَتَيْنِ صَدْرًا مِنْ خِلَافَتِهِ، ثُمَّ أَتَمَّهَا بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ

Transmission path

  2. 02Salimسالم
  3. 03al-Zuhriالزهري
  4. 04'Abbad b. Ishaqعباد بن اسحاق
  5. 05Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

Entry155/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 196
English
Isnad

From Musa b. 'Uqba, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said:

Matn

When the Messenger of God ﷺ made tawaf in Hajj or 'Umra, the first time he arrived he would trot three circuits from the start of his tawaf and walk four circuits, then pray two rak'as, then circumambulate between al-Safa and al-Marwa.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ:

Matn

كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ إِذَا طَافَ فِي الْحَجِّ أَوِ الْعُمْرَةِ أَوَّلُ مَا يَقْدِمُ «يَسْعَى ثَلَاثَةَ طَوَافَاتٍ مِنْ أَوَّلِ طَوَافِهِ، وَيَمْشِي أَرْبَعَةَ أَطْوَافٍ مَشْيًا، ثُمَّ يُصَلِّي رَكْعَتَيْنِ، ثُمَّ يَطُوفُ بَيْنَ الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةِ»

Transmission path

  1. 01'Abd Allah b. 'UmarEarliest transmitterابن عمر
  2. 02Nafi'نافع
  3. 03Musa b. 'Uqbaموسي بن عقبه

English
Isnad

From Yasin, from 'Amr b. Dinar, from al-Miswar b. Makhrama, that he said:

Matn

A man came to the Messenger of God ﷺ and said: “I performed two sevens [of tawaf], joined them together, and prayed four rak'as.” He said: “You did well.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ يَاسِينَ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنِ الْمِسْوَرِ بْنِ مَخْرَمَةَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

جَاءَ رَجُلٌ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ فَقَالَ: إِنِّي طُفْتُ سَبْعَيْنِ، فَقَرَنْتُ بَيْنَهُمَا، وَرَكَعَتْ أَرْبَعَ رَكَعَاتٍ؟ قَالَ: «أَحْسَنْتَ»

Entry157/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 196-200
English
Isnad

From Ayyub (al-Sakhtiyani), from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar:

Matn

that a man asked the Messenger of God ﷺ: “What does one in ihram leave off of clothing?” or he said: “What may one in ihram wear of clothing?” The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “He does not wear a shirt, a turban, trousers or leather socks, unless he finds no sandals; if he finds no sandals, let him wear socks cut below the ankles; and he does not wear a garment touched by wars or saffron, and does not wear the burnous.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ أَيُّوبَ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَجُلًا، سَأَلَ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ فَقَالَ: مَا يَتْرُكُ الْمُحْرِمُ مِنَ الثِّيَابِ؟ أَوْ قَالَ: مَا يَلْبَسُ الْمُحْرِمُ مِنَ الثِّيَابِ؟ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «لَا يَلْبَسُ الْقَمِيصَ، وَلَا الْعِمَامَةَ، وَلَا السَّرَاوِيلَ، وَلَا الْخُفَّيْنِ، إِلَّا أَنْ لَا يَجِدَ نَعْلَيْنِ، فَإِنْ لَمْ يَجِدْ نَعْلَيْنِ فَلْيَلْبِسَ الْخُفَّيْنِ أَسْفَلَ مِنَ الْكَعْبَيْنِ، وَلَا يَلْبَسُ ثَوْبًا مَسَّهُ الْوَرْسُ وَالزَّعْفَرَانُ، وَلَا يَلْبَسُ الْبُرْنُسُ»

Transmission path

  1. 01'Abd Allah b. 'UmarEarliest transmitterابن عمر
  2. 02Nafi'نافع
  3. 03Ayyub (al-Sakhtiyani)ايوب
  4. 04Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

From al-Hajjaj b. al-Hajjaj, from 'Abd al-Rahman b. al-Qasim, from his father (not named in the text), from 'A'isha, who said:

Matn

I used to perfume the Messenger of God ﷺ at his entering ihram when he entered it, and at his release when he threw the pebbles, before he visited the House.

العربية
Isnad

Matn

كُنْتُ أُطَيِّبُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ عِنْدَ إِحْرَامِهِ حِينَ يَحْرُمُ، وَعِنْدَ حِلِّهِ حِينَ يَرْمِي الْجَمْرَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَزُورَ الْبَيْتَ»

Entry161/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 200-203
English
Isnad

From Sufyan, from Salama b. Kuhayl, from al-Hasan al-'Urani, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Abbas, who said:

Matn

Whoever throws the farthest jamra on the Day of Sacrifice, everything becomes lawful for him except women. It was said to him: “What of perfume?” He said: “I saw the Messenger of God ﷺ with his head anointed with musk; do you think that was perfume or not?”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ سُفْيَانَ، عَنْ سَلَمَةَ بْنِ كُهَيْلٍ، عَنِ الْحَسَنِ الْعُرَنِيِّ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ:

Matn

مَنْ رَمَى الْجَمْرَةَ الْقُصْوَى يَوْمَ النَّحْرِ فَقَدْ حَلَّ لَهُ كُلُّ شَيْءٍ إِلَّا النِّسَاءَ. فَقِيلَ لَهُ: فَمَا شَأْنُ الطِّيبِ؟ فَقَالَ: لَقَدْ رَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ «قَدْ ضَمَخَ رَأْسَهُ بِالْمِسْكِ، أَفَتَرَى ذَلِكَ كَانَ طِيبًا أَمْ لَا»

Entry164/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 203
English
Isnad

From Musa b. 'Uqba, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said:

Matn

When the Messenger of God ﷺ came to Mecca he would stop at Dhu Tuwa and spend the night until he prayed the dawn prayer. Ibn 'Umar said: The place of prayer of the Messenger of God ﷺ at that spot is on a rough hillock, not where the mosque was built, but below that.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ:

Matn

كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ «إِذَا قَدِمَ مَكَّةَ نَزَلَ بِذِي طُوًى، وَيَبِيتُ حَتَّى يُصَلِّيَ صَلَاةَ الصُّبْحِ، قَالَ ابْنُ عُمَرَ: وَمُصَلَّى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ بِذَلِكَ الْمَكَانِ عَلَى أَكَمَةٍ غَلِيظَةٍ، لَيْسَ حَيْثُ بَنِي الْمَسْجِدَ، وَلَكِنْ أَسْفَلَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ»

  2. 02Nafi'نافع
  3. 03Musa b. 'Uqbaموسي بن عقبه

Entry165/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 203
English
Isnad

From Malik b. Anas, from Zayd b. Aslam, from Ibrahim b. 'Abd Allah b. Hunayn, from his father (not named in the text), that he said:

Matn

that 'Abd Allah b. 'Abbas and al-Miswar b. Makhrama disputed while at al-Abwa'. Ibn 'Abbas said: “The one in ihram may wash his head,” and al-Miswar said: “He may not wash it.” Ibn 'Abbas sent me to Abu Ayyub al-Ansari to ask him about that hadith. [The hadith continues; abbreviated in the source.]

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ أَنَسٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ حُنَيْنٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

إِنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَبَّاسٍ، وَالْمِسْوَرَ بْنَ مَخْرَمَةَ تَمَارَيَا وَهُمَا بِالْأَبْوَاءِ، فَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ: " يَغْسِلُ الْمُحْرِمُ رَأْسَهُ، وَقَالَ الْمِسْوَرُ لَا يَغْسِلُهُ. فَأَرْسَلَنِي ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ إِلَى أَبِي أَيُّوبَ الْأَنْصَارِيِّ أَسْأَلُهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ الْحَدِيثِ

Entry167/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 203-206
English
Isnad

From al-Mughira, from al-Sha'bi, from Ka'b b. 'Ujra, who said:

Matn

By Him in whose hand is my soul, this verse was revealed concerning me, and it was I whom it meant. I came to the Messenger of God ﷺ on the day of al-Hudaybiyya and he said: “Do your lice trouble you?” I said: “Yes.” He said: So God Almighty revealed: {and whoever of you is ill or has an ailment of the head, then a ransom of fasting or charity or sacrifice} [al-Baqara 2:196]. The fast is three days, the food is for six poor persons, three sa's, half a sa' for each poor person, and the sacrifice is a sheep.

العربية
Isnad

عَنِ الْمُغِيرَةِ، عَنِ الشَّعْبِيِّ، عَنْ كَعْبِ بْنِ عُجْرَةَ، قَالَ:

Matn

وَالَّذِي نَفْسِي بِيَدِهِ قَدْ «أُنْزِلَتْ هَذِهِ الْآيَةُ وَلَإِيَّايَ عُنِيَ بِهَا، أَتَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ يَوْمَ الْحُدَيْبِيَةِ فَقَالَ:» أَيُؤْذِيكَ هَوَامُّكَ؟ «فَقُلْتُ: نَعَمْ. قَالَ: فَأَنْزَلَ اللَّهُ عز وجل {فَمَنْ كَانَ مِنْكُمْ مَرِيضًا أَوْ بِهِ أَذًى مِنْ رَأْسِهِ فَفِدْيَةٌ مِنْ صِيَامٍ أَوْ صَدَقَةٍ أَوْ نُسُكٍ} [البقرة: 196] الصِّيَامُ ثَلَاثَةُ أَيَّامٍ، وَالطَّعَامُ لِسِتَّةِ مَسَاكِينَ ثَلَاثَةُ أَصْوُعٍ لِكُلِّ مِسْكِينٍ نِصْفُ صَاعٍ، وَالنُّسُكُ شَاةٌ»

  2. 02al-Sha'biالشعبي
  3. 03al-Mughiraالمغيره

Entry168/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 206
English
Isnad

From Malik b. Anas, from 'Abd al-Karim al-Jazari, from Mujahid, from 'Abd al-Rahman b. Abi Layla, from Ka'b b. 'Ujra:

Matn

that he was with the Messenger of God ﷺ in a state of ihram and lice troubled him on his head; the Messenger of God ﷺ ordered him to shave his head and said: “Fast three days, or feed six poor persons, two mudds each, or sacrifice a sheep; whichever of these you do will suffice for you.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ أَنَسٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْكَرِيمِ الْجَزَرِيِّ، عَنْ مُجَاهِدٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ أَبِي لَيْلَى، عَنْ كَعْبِ بْنِ عُجْرَةَ، أَنَّهُ حَدَّثَهُ

Matn

أَنَّهُ كَانَ مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ مُحْرِمًا فَآذَاهُ الْقُمَّلُ فِي رَأْسِهِ، فَأَمَرَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ بِحَلْقِ رَأْسِهِ وَقَالَ: «صُمْ ثَلَاثَةَ أَيَّامٍ أَوْ أَطْعِمْ سِتَّةَ مَسَاكِينَ مُدَّيْنِ مُدَّيْنِ، أَوْ أَنْسِكْ شَاةً، أَيُّ ذَلِكَ فَعَلْتَ أَجْزَأَ عَنْكَ»

English
Isnad

From Hisham al-Dastuwa'i, from Yahya b. Abi Kathir, from Abu Ibrahim, from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri, that he said:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ and his Companions shaved in the year of al-Hudaybiyya, except 'Uthman and Abu Qatada; and the Messenger of God ﷺ asked forgiveness for those who shaved three times, and for those who shortened once.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ هِشَامٍ الدَّسْتُوَائِيِّ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ أَبِي كَثِيرٍ، عَنْ أَبِي إِبْرَاهِيمَ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ أَنَّهُ قَالَ: «

Matn

حَلَقَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ وَأَصْحَابُهُ عَامَ الْحُدَيْبِيَةِ غَيْرَ عُثْمَانَ، وَأَبِي قَتَادَةَ، وَاسْتَغْفَرَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ لِلْمُحَلِّقِينَ ثَلَاثًا، وَلِلْمُقَصِّرِينَ مَرَّةً»

Entry170/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 206
English
Isnad

From Mutarrif [Matar], from Qatada, from 'Ikrima, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Abbas, who said:

Matn

The wife of Abu Bakr al-Siddiq gave birth while in ihram, and she was ordered to perform what the pilgrim performs, except that she should not circumambulate the House nor between al-Safa and al-Marwa; when she became pure she circumambulated the House and between al-Safa and al-Marwa.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مَطَرٍ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ عِكْرِمَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ: «

Matn

وَلَدَتِ امْرَأَةُ أَبِي بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقِ وَهِيَ مُحْرِمَةٌ، فَأُمِرَتْ أَنْ تَقْضِيَ مَا يَقْضِي الْحَاجُّ غَيْرَ أَنْ لَا تَطُوفَ بِالْبَيْتِ وَلَا بَيْنَ الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةِ، فَإِذَا طَهُرَتْ طَافَتْ بِالْبَيْتِ وَبَيْنَ الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةِ»

Entry171/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 206-209
English
Isnad

From Malik b. Anas, from 'Abd al-Rahman, from al-Qasim, from 'A'isha, who said:

Matn

Safiyya daughter of Huyayy menstruated while we were at Mina, and that was mentioned to the Messenger of God ﷺ, who said: “Is she going to detain us?” It was said: “She has already made the Ifada [tawaf].” He said: “Then no [problem].”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ أَنَسٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، قَالَتْ

Matn

حَاضَتْ صَفِيَّةُ ابْنَةُ حُيَيٍّ وَنَحْنُ بِمِنًى، فَذُكِرَ ذَلِكَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ فَقَالَ: «أَحَابِسَتُنَا هِيَ؟» فَقِيلَ: إِنَّهَا قَدْ أَفَاضَتْ. قَالَ: «فَلَا إِذًا»

Entry172/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 209-210
English
Isnad

From 'Abbad b. Ishaq, from Muhammad b. Muslim al-Zuhri, from 'Urwa b. al-Zubayr and Abu Salama, from 'A'isha, who said:

Matn

Safiyya daughter of Huyayy menstruated after she had made the Ifada, and that was mentioned to the Messenger of God ﷺ, who said: “Is she going to detain us?” She said: I said: “O Messenger of God, she has already made the Ifada and circumambulated the House, then menstruated after the Ifada.” She said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Then let her depart.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ عَبَّادِ بْنِ إِسْحَاقَ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ مُسْلِمٍ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ، وَأَبِي سَلَمَةَ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، قَالَتْ:

Matn

حَاضَتْ صَفِيَّةُ ابْنَةُ حُيَيٍّ بَعْدَ مَا أَفَاضَتْ، فَذُكِرَ ذَلِكَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ فَقَالَ: «أَحَابِسَتُنَا هِيَ؟» قَالَتْ: فَقُلْتُ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ إِنَّهَا قَدْ أَفَاضَتْ، وَطَافَتْ بِالْبَيْتِ، ثُمَّ حَاضَتْ بَعْدَ الْإِفَاضَةِ؟ قَالَتْ: فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: فَلْتَنْفِرْ "

English
Isnad

From Yahya b. Sa'id, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, from Zayd b. Thabit:

Matn

that the Messenger of God ﷺ permitted 'araya [sales]: the people of a house take [fresh fruit on the tree] by its estimate in dates, eating it as fresh dates.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ ثَابِتٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ «

Matn

رَخَّصَ فِي الْعَرَايَا، يَأْخُذُهَا أَهْلُ الْبَيْتِ بِخَرْصِهَا تَمْرًا، يَأْكُلُونَهُ رُطَبًا»

Entry175/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 210
English
Isnad

From Sa'id, from Yahya b. Subayh, from 'Amr b. Dinar, from Muhammad b. 'Ali Abu Ja'far, from Ka'b b. Malik al-Ansari:

Matn

[lacuna in the source] ... while he was pursuing a man until midday. The Messenger of God ﷺ signaled with his hand thus to Ka'b, gesturing to him: “Would you take [part of] the debt, since the debt is your right?” as though he signaled half. He said: “Yes.” He said: “Then come [and take] what remains of it.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ صُبَيْحٍ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَلِيٍّ أَبِي جَعْفَرٍ، عَنْ كَعْبِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ الْأَنْصَارِيِّ،. ..

Matn

وَهُوَ يُجَارِي رَجُلًا حَتَّى انْتَصَفَ النَّهَارُ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ بِيَدِهِ هَكَذَا لْكَعْبٍ يُومِئُ إِلَيْهِ: «هَلْ لَكَ فِي الدِّينِ وَالدَّيْنِ مِنْ حَقِّكَ؟» كَأَنَّهُ أَوْمَى بِالشَّطْرِ قَالَ: نَعَمْ. قَالَ: «هَلُمَّ إِلَى مَا غَبَرَ مِنْهُ»

Transmission path

Isnad

By the same chain as report 177: from Musa b. 'Uqba, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said:

Matn

Isnad

English
Isnad

From 'Abbad b. Ishaq, from al-Zuhri, from Salim, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Whoever sells a slave, his property belongs to the seller, unless the buyer stipulates [otherwise]; and whoever sells palm trees after they have been pollinated, their fruit belongs to the seller, unless the buyer stipulates.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ عَبَّادِ بْنِ إِسْحَاقَ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

مَنْ بَاعَ عَبْدًا فَمَالُهُ لِلَّذِي بَاعَهُ إِلَّا أَنْ يَشْتَرِطَ الْمُبْتَاعُ، وَمَنْ بَاعَ نَخْلًا بَعْدَ أَنْ تُؤُبَّرَ فَثَمَرَتُهَا لِلَّذِي بَاعَهَا إِلَّا أَنْ يَشْتَرِطَ الْمُبْتَاعُ»

Entry180/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 210
English
Isnad

From Musa b. 'Uqba, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The two parties to a sale have the option regarding their sale as long as they have not separated, or if their sale was with an option; if their sale was with an option, the sale is binding.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

الْبَيِّعَانِ بِالْخِيَارِ مِنْ بَيْعِهِمَا مَا لَمْ يَفْتَرِقَا، أَوْ يَكُونُ بَيْعُهُمَا عَنْ خِيَارٍ، فَإِذَا كَانَ بَيْعُهُمَا عَنْ خِيَارٍ فَقَدْ وَجَبَ الْبَيْعُ»

Transmission path

  1. 01'Abd Allah b. 'UmarEarliest transmitterابن عمر
  2. 02Nafi'نافع
  3. 03Musa b. 'Uqbaموسي بن عقبه

From al-Hasan b. 'Umara, from Muhammad b. Muslim al-Zuhri, from Sa'id b. al-Musayyab, from Ma'mar b. 'Abd Allah, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“No one hoards except a sinner.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنِ الْحَسَنِ بْنِ عُمَارَةَ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ مُسْلِمٍ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، عَنْ مَعْمَرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

لَا يَحْتَكِرُ إِلَّا خَاطِئٌ»

English
Isnad

From Khalid al-Hadhdha', from 'Ata' b. Abi Rabah, from Hakim b. Hizam, who said:

Matn

I used to buy food, and the Messenger of God ﷺ forbade me to sell it until I had taken possession of it.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ خَالِدٍ الْحَذَّاءِ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ أَبِي رَبَاحٍ، عَنْ حَكِيمِ بْنِ حِزَامٍ، قَالَ:

Matn

كُنْتُ أَشْتَرِي الطَّعَامَ فَنَهَانِي رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ أَنْ أَبِيعَهُ حَتَّى أَقْبِضَهُ "

English
Isnad

From 'Ubayd Allah b. 'Umar al-'Umari, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Let none of you sell over his brother's sale, nor propose over his brother's proposal, until he permits him.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ الْعُمَرِيِّ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

لَا يَبِيعُ أَحَدُكُمْ عَلَى بَيْعِ أَخِيهِ، وَلَا يَخْطُبُ عَلَى خِطْبَةِ أَخِيهِ، حَتَّى يَأْذَنَ لَهُ»

Entry186/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 219-221
English
Isnad

From al-Hajjaj b. al-Hajjaj, from Qatada, from Anas b. Malik, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Friends are three: one friend says: 'Yours is what you gave, and what you withheld is not yours'; that is his wealth. Another friend says: 'I am with you until you reach the door of the king, then I return and leave you'; that is his family and his clan, who escort him until he reaches his grave, then return and leave him. And another friend says: 'I am with you wherever you enter and wherever you go out'; that is his deed. He says: 'By God, I held you the lightest of the three.'”

العربية
Isnad

عَنِ الْحَجَّاجِ بْنِ الْحَجَّاجِ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، قَالَ: قَالَ النَّبِيُّ ﷺ: «

Matn

الْأَخِلَّاءُ ثَلَاثَةٌ: فَأَمَّا خَلِيلٌ، فَيَقُولُ: لَكَ مَا أَعْطَيْتَهُ، وَمَا أَمْسَكْتَ فَلَيْسَ لَكَ، ذَلِكَ مَالُهُ، وَأَمَّا خَلِيلٌ ، فَيَقُولُ: أَنَا مَعَكَ حَتَّى تَأْتِيَ بَابَ الْمَلِكِ، ثُمَّ أَرْجِعُ وَأَتْرُكُكَ، وَذَلِكَ أَهْلُهُ وَعَشِيرَتُهُ يُشَيِّعُونُهُ حَتَّى يَأْتِيَ قَبْرَهُ، ثُمَّ يَرْجِعُونَ وَيَتْرُكُونَهُ، وَأَمَّا خَلِيلٌ، فَيَقُولُ: أَنَا مَعَكُ حَيْثُ دَخَلْتَ، وَحَيْثُ خَرَجْتَ، فَذَلِكَ عَمَلُهُ، فَيَقُولُ: وَاللَّهِ لَقَدْ كُنْتَ مِنْ أَهْوَنِ الثَّلَاثَةِ عَلَيَّ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

  1. 01Anas b. MalikEarliest transmitterانس
  2. 02Qatadaقتاده
  3. 03al-Hajjaj b. al-Hajjajالحجاج بن الحجاج

English
Isnad

From al-Hajjaj b. al-Hajjaj, from Qatada, from al-Hasan, from Jundab b. 'Abd Allah, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

العربية

Entry188/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 221
English
Isnad

From Aban b. Abi 'Ayyash, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar:

Matn

that some people said: “O Abu 'Abd al-Rahman, what prevents you from desiring what people desire of this leadership?” He said: “After the Messenger of God's words ﷺ: 'A man will be called to account on the Day of Resurrection until he is asked about his child and his wife'? I have not desired anything after this.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ أَبَانَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبَّاسٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ،

Matn

أَنَّ أُنَاسًا، قَالُوا: يَا أَبَا عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، مَا يَمْنَعُكَ أَنْ تَرْغَبَ، فِيمَا يَرْغَبُ فِيهِ النَّاسُ مِنْ هَذِهِ الْإِمَارَةِ؟ قَالَ: أَبْعَدُ قَوْلِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ سَلَّمَ أَنَّهُ قَالَ: إِنَّ «الرَّجُلَ لَيُحَاسَبُ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ حَتَّى يُسْأَلَ عَنْ وَلَدِهِ، وَزَوْجَتِهِ» فَمَا رَغِبْتُ فِيمَا بَعْدَ هَذَا

Transmission path

Entry189/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 221
English
Isnad

From al-Hasan, from Habib b. Abi Thabit, from al-Qasim b. Muhammad b. 'Abd al-Rahman b. al-Harith b. Hisham, from 'Ubayd Allah b. 'Abd Allah b. 'Utba b. Mas'ud, from Abu Mas'ud al-Ansari, who said:

Matn

We were with the Messenger of God ﷺ in a house, and he said: “This matter will remain among you and you will be its custodians so long as you do not introduce a deed that God removes from you; when you do that, God will set over you the worst of His creation, who will strip you as a stick is stripped of its bark.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنِ الْحَسَنِ، عَنْ حَبِيبِ بْنِ أَبِي ثَابِتٍ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ هِشَامٍ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، عَنْ أَبِي مَسْعُودٍ الْأَنْصَارِيِّ، قَالَ:

Matn

كُنَّا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ فِي بَيْتٍ فَقَالَ: «إِنَّ هَذَا الْأَمْرَ لَا يَزَالُ فِيكُمْ وَأَنْتُمْ وُلَاتُهُ مَا لَمْ تُحْدِثُوا عَمَلًا يَنْزِعُهُ اللَّهُ فِيكُمْ، فَإِذَا فَعَلْتُمْ ذَلِكَ، سَلَّطَ اللَّهُ عَلَيْكُمْ شِرَارَ خَلْقِهِ فَالْتَحَوْكُمْ كَمَا يُلْتَحَى الْقَضِيبُ»

  1. 01Abu Mas'ud al-AnsariEarliest transmitterابي مسعود الانصاري
  2. 02'Ubayd Allah b. 'Abd Allah b. 'Utba b. Mas'udعبيد الله بن عبد الله بن عتبه بن مسعود

Entry190/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 221-224
English
Isnad

From Muhammad b. Maysara, from al-Zuhri, from Ibn al-Musayyab:

Matn

that a woman was brought before 'Umar b. al-Khattab, asking for her inheritance from the blood-money of her husband. 'Umar said: “The blood-money belongs only to the 'aqila [the paternal kin group]; I know nothing for you.” Al-Dahhak b. Sufyan said: “I bear witness that the Messenger of God ﷺ wrote to me: 'Let the wife of Ashyam al-Dibabi inherit from the blood-money of her husband.'” So 'Umar gave her the inheritance.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ مَيْسَرَةَ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنِ ابْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، أَنَّهُ حَدَّثَ

Matn

أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ رُفِعَتْ إِلَيْهِ امْرَأَةٌ تَطْلُبُ مِيرَاثَهَا مِنْ دِيَةِ زَوْجِهَا، فَقَالَ عُمَرُ: إِنَّمَا الدِّيَةُ لِلْعَاقِلَةِ فَلَا أَعْلَمُ لَكِ شَيْئًا. فَقَالَ الضَّحَّاكُ بْنُ سُفْيَانَ: أَشْهَدُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ﷺ كَتَبَ إِلَيَّ أَنْ «أُوَرِّثَ امْرَأَةَ أَشْيَمَ الضِّبَابِيِّ مِنْ دِيَةِ زَوْجِهَا» فَوَرَّثَهَا عُمَرُ "

Transmission path

  1. 01Ibn al-MusayyabEarliest transmitterابن المسيب
  2. 02al-Zuhriالزهري
  3. 03Muhammad b. Maysaraمحمد بن ميسره
  4. 04Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

Entry191/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 224
English
Isnad

From 'Abd al-'Aziz b. 'Abd Allah b. Abi Salama, from al-Zuhri, from Abu Salama, from Abu Hurayra:

Matn

that a dispute was brought to the Messenger of God ﷺ about a woman who struck another while she was pregnant, so that she miscarried; the Messenger of God ﷺ ruled regarding her fetus a ghurra: a male or female slave.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّهُ أَخْبَرَهُ

Matn

أَنَّهُ، اخْتَصَمَ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ فِي امْرَأَةٍ رَمَتْ أُخْرَى وَهِيَ حُبْلَى، فَأَسْقَطَتْ، «فَقَضَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ فِي جَنِينِهَا غُرَّةٌ عَبْدٌ أَوْ أَمَةٌ»

Entry192/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 224
English
Isnad

From Khalid al-Hadhdha', from al-Qasim b. 'Abd al-Rahman, from 'Uqba b. 'Amir, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said on the day of the conquest of Mecca:

Matn

“There is no god but God alone; He fulfilled His promise, gave victory to His servant, and defeated the confederates alone.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ خَالِدٍ الْحَذَّاءِ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ عُقْبَةَ بْنِ عَامِرٍ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ يَوْمَ فَتْحِ مَكَّةَ: «

Matn

لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ، صَدَقَ وَعْدَهُ، وَنَصَرَ عَبْدَهُ، وَهَزَمَ الْأَحْزَابَ وَحْدَهُ»

Entry193/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 224
English
Isnad

From Ayyub b. Abi Tamima, from Abu al-Zubayr, from Jabir, who said:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ sent three hundred men and appointed Abu 'Ubayda b. al-Jarrah over them. He said: He provisioned them with a bag of dates, and Abu 'Ubayda would provide for them, at first, a handful for each man. He mentioned the hadith. [Abbreviated in the source.]

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ أَيُّوبَ بْنِ أَبِي تَمِيمَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ جَابِرٍ، قَالَ: "

Matn

بَعَثَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ ثَلَاثَمِائَةِ رَجُلٍ وَأَمَّرَ عَلَيْهِمْ أَبَا عُبَيْدَةَ بْنَ الْجَرَّاحِ، قَالَ: وَزَوَّدَهُمْ جِرَابَ تَمْرٍ، فَكَانَ أَبُو عُبَيْدَةَ يَرْزُقُهُمْ أَوَّلَ مَرَّةٍ قَبْضَةً قَبْضَةً كُلَّ رَجُلٍ فَذَكَرَ الْحَدِيثَ

English
Isnad

From Husayn, from Yahya b. Abi Kathir, from 'Ikrima, from 'Abd Allah b. 'Abbas, who said:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ would combine the noon and afternoon prayers on a journey, when he was on the move, and combine the sunset and night prayers.

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ حُسَيْنٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ أَبِي كَثِيرٍ، عَنْ عِكْرِمَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ:

Matn

كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ «يَجْمَعُ بَيْنَ صَلَاةِ الظُّهْرِ وَالْعَصْرِ فِي السَّفَرِ، إِذَا كَانَ عَلَى ظَهْرِ سَيْرٍ، وَيَجْمَعُ بَيْنَ الْمَغْرِبِ وَالْعِشَاءِ»

Entry196/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 228-229
English
Isnad

From Abu al-Juwayriya, from 'Asal, from 'Ata' b. Abi Rabah, from Abu Hurayra, that he said:

Matn

“The star (al-najm) has never risen in the morning over a people or a town in which there was a blight but it lightened or was lifted from them.” I said: “From whom is this, O Abu Muhammad?” He said: “From Abu Hurayra, from the Messenger of God ﷺ.”

العربية
Isnad

عَنْ أَبِي الْجُوَيْرِيَةِ، عَنْ عَسَلٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ أَبِي رَبَاحٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ: «

Matn

مَا طَلَعَ النَّجْمُ غَدَاةً قَطُّ وَبِقَوْمٍ أَوْ بِقَرْيَةٍ عَاهَةٌ، إِلَّا خَفَّتْ أَوِ ارْتَفَعَتْ عَنْهُمْ» فَقُلْتُ: عَمَّنْ هَذَا يَا أَبَا مُحَمَّدٍ؟ قَالَ: عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ

Isnad

From Muhammad b. Maysara, from al-Zuhri, from Sa'id b. al-Musayyab, from al-Bara' b. 'Azib:

Matn

that a she-camel of his had entered the orchard of some people and damaged it, so the Messenger of God ﷺ ruled concerning it that the owners of orchards must guard by day, and the owners of livestock must guard by night.

عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

مَنْ شَرِبَ الْخَمْرَ فِي الدُّنْيَا لَمْ يَشْرَبْهَا فِي الْآخِرَةِ إِلَّا أَنْ يَتُوبَ»

Transmission overview

Transmission path

  1. 01'Abd Allah b. 'UmarEarliest transmitterابن عمر
  2. 02Nafi'نافع
  3. 03Musa b. 'Uqbaموسي بن عقبه
  4. 04Ibrahim b. TahmanCompilerإبراهيم بن طهمان

Entry204/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 234
English
Isnad

By the same chain as report 203: from Musa b. 'Uqba, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“When one of you is at his meal, let him not hurry away from it until he has finished his need of it, even if the prayer is called.”

العربية
Isnad

وَبِهِ قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

إِذَا كَانَ أَحَدُكُمْ عَلَى طَعَامِهِ، فَلَا يَعْجَلَنَّ عَنْهُ حَتَّى يَقْضِيَ حَاجَتَهُ مِنْهُ، وَإِنْ أُقِيمَتِ الصَّلَاةُ»

English
Isnad

By the same chain as report 203: from Musa b. 'Uqba, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, that:

Matn

that the Messenger of God ﷺ ordered that zakat al-fitr be paid before the people go out to the prayer.

العربية
Isnad

Matn

Entry206/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 234
English
Isnad

By the same chain as report 203: from Musa b. 'Uqba, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The two parties to a sale have the option regarding their sale as long as they have not separated, or if their sale was with an option; if their sale was with an option, the sale is binding – or they separate.”

العربية
Isnad

وَبِهِ قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ: «

Matn

الْبَيِّعَانِ بِالْخِيَارِ مِنْ بَيْعِهِمَا مَا لَمْ يَتَفَرَّقَا، أَوْ يَكُونُ بَيْعُهُمَا عَنْ خِيَارٍ، فَإِذَا كَانَ بَيْعُهُمَا عَنْ خِيَارٍ فَقَدْ وَجَبَ الْبَيْعُ أَوْ يَتَفَرَّقَا»

  2. 02Nafi'نافع
  3. 03Musa b. 'Uqbaموسي بن عقبه

English
Isnad

By the same chain as report 203: from Musa b. 'Uqba, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, who said:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ used to observe i'tikaf during the first ten [days] of Ramadan.

Isnad

Matn

Entry208/ 208
Mashyakhat Ibn Ṭahmān · p. 234
English
Isnad

By the same chain as report 203: from Musa b. 'Uqba, from Nafi', from 'Abd Allah b. 'Umar, that he said:

Matn

“The Jews brought to the Messenger of God ﷺ a man and a woman of theirs who had committed adultery. The Messenger of God ﷺ said to them: 'What do you find in the Torah?' They said: 'We find nothing.' 'Abd Allah b. Salam said: 'You lie; in the Torah is stoning. Bring the Torah and recite it, if you are truthful.' They brought the Torah and spread it; the scholar among them who studies it placed his palm over the verse of stoning and began to read what was before his hand and after it, not reading the verse of stoning. 'Abd Allah b. Salam pulled his hand off the verse of stoning and said: 'What is this?' When they saw that, he said: 'It is the verse of stoning.' He said: The Messenger of God ﷺ ordered the two to be stoned, near where the funerals are placed by the mosque.” 'Abd Allah said: I saw the man bending over her, shielding her from the stones.

العربية
Isnad

وَبِهِ أَنَّهُ قَالَ: «

Matn

إِنَّ الْيَهُودَ جَاءُوا بِرَجُلٍ مِنْهُمْ وَامْرَأَةٍ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ﷺ قَدْ زَنَيَا، فَقَالَ لَهُمْ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ:» فَمَا تَجِدُونَ فِي التَّوْرَاةِ؟ «فَقَالُوا: لَا نَجِدُ شَيْئًا. فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَلَامٍ: كَذَبْتُمْ، فِي التَّوْرَاةِ الرَّجْمُ، فَأْتُوا بِالتَّوْرَاةِ فَاتْلُوهَا إِنْ كُنْتُمْ صَادِقِينَ. فَجَاءُوا بِالتَّوْرَاةِ، فَوَضَعُوهَا، فَوَضَعَ مِدْرَاسُهَا الَّذِي يَدْرُسُهَا مِنْهُمْ كَفَّهُ عَلَى آيَةِ الرَّجْمِ، فَطَفِقَ يَقْرَأُ مَا دُونَ يَدِهِ وَمَا وَرَاءَهَا، وَلَا يَقْرَأُ آيَةَ الرَّجْمِ، قَالَ: فَنَزَعَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ سَلَامٍ يَدَهُ عَنْ آيَةِ الرَّجْمِ، فَقَالَ: مَا هَذِهِ؟ فَلَمَّا رَأَوْا ذَلِكَ، قَالَ: هِيَ آيَةُ الرَّجْمِ. قَالَ:» فَأَمَرَ بِهِمَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ فَرُجِمَا قَرِيبًا مِنْ حَيْثُ يُوضَعُ الْجَنَائِزُ عِنْدَ الْمَسْجِدِ " فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ: فَرَأَيْتُ الرَّجُلَ يَحْنَأُ عَلَيْهَا يَقِيهَا الْحِجَارَةَ

  3. 03Musa b. 'Uqbaموسي بن عقبه
